## 432. Exact Long-Press and Rapid-Tap Sequences as Real-Vosk Stress Tests (Live Code)

**Long-press (mobile, from source):**
```js
await ember.dispatchEvent('pointerdown');
await page.waitForTimeout(700);
await ember.dispatchEvent('pointerup');
await ember.click({ force: true });
...
assert(longPress.states.every(s => s === 'idle'), ...);
assert(longPress.calls === beforeLong, 'long press stop does not restart speech');
```

**Rapid taps:**
```js
for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) await ember.click({ force: true });
...
assert(afterRapid.calls <= beforeRapid + 3, 'rapid taps do not create runaway duplicate utterances');
assert(afterRapid.states.every(s => ['idle','playing','paused','complete'].includes(s)), ...);
```

**Real Vosk value (when --real-tts):**
- Long-press exercises `cancelActiveEngine()` → real `VoskTTSEngine.cancel(handle)` while synthesis may be in flight or model still loading.
- Rapid taps exercise multiple overlapping `handlePlayClick` → runId increments + stale callbacks being suppressed by `cancelled` flag and runId checks.
- Both exercise Blob URL revoke under concurrent operations on long Gill content.
- Both exercise state broadcast to multiple embers (rail topbar + mobile bottom bar + any other).

Currently these are only validated against the fake. The real path will be the first automated validation of the heavy async machinery under realistic user gestures on the pages that matter most.

## 441. Gill Smoke — Long-Press Stop + Rapid Taps as Real-Vosk Validator (Live Source Analysis)

**Script:** `scripts/gill-v16-mobile-play-smoke.js` (fetched live 2026-07-08, chunk 3)

**Long-press stop test (mobile only, verbatim key parts):**
```js
const beforeLong = await page.evaluate(() => window.__ttsFake.speakCalls);
await ember.dispatchEvent('pointerdown');
await page.waitForTimeout(700);
await ember.dispatchEvent('pointerup');
await ember.click({ force: true }); // emulate the follow-up click generated after long press
await page.waitForTimeout(250);
const longPress = await page.evaluate(() => ({
  calls: window.__ttsFake.speakCalls,
  states: [...document.querySelectorAll('.gb-ember')].map(el => el.dataset.state),
  cancels: window.__ttsFake.cancelCalls
}));
assert(longPress.states.every(s => s === 'idle'), `${label}: long press stop/reset stays idle after pointerup/click cycle`);
assert(longPress.calls === beforeLong, `${label}: long press stop does not restart speech`);
```

**Rapid taps guard (5 forced clicks):**
```js
const beforeRapid = await page.evaluate(() => window.__ttsFake.speakCalls);
for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) await ember.click({ force: true });
await page.waitForTimeout(300);
const afterRapid = await page.evaluate(() => ({
  calls: window.__ttsFake.speakCalls,
  states: [...document.querySelectorAll('.gb-ember')].map(el => el.dataset.state),
  events: window.__ttsFake.events
}));
assert(afterRapid.calls <= beforeRapid + 3, `${label}: rapid taps do not create runaway duplicate utterances`);
assert(afterRapid.states.every(s => ['idle', 'playing', 'paused', 'complete'].includes(s)), ...);
```

**When the smoke is run with `--real-tts` (skipping addFakeTts):**
- The real `VoskTTSEngine.speak(text, rate, speakerId, onend, onerror)` is called.
- It returns a handle `{ engine: 'vosk', cancelled: false }`.
- Controller stores it as `ttsState.utterance`.
- Long-press → `cancelActiveEngine()` → `VoskTTSEngine.cancel(ttsState.utterance)`.
- Real `cancel(handle)` sets `handle.cancelled = true`, does `audioEl.pause()`, removes src, `load()`, revokes `currentObjectUrl`.
- Controller's `runId` + `suppressEnd` must suppress any in-flight `synthChunk` promises and prevent state advancement.
- All embers (including Gill rail topbar PlayEmber and mobile bottom bar) must reach 'idle' and clear `--p`.
- Rapid clicks must not cause multiple overlapping `ensureLoaded()` + multiple synth sessions due to the runId guard.

These two tests in the existing smoke are currently the **best automated validators** for the exact race-protection and cleanup logic that protects the real async neural path on long Gill articles.

## 442. synthChunk + injectCustomStress + Multistream/BERT (Live Engine + Smoke Value)

From `js/vosk-tts-engine.js` (live fetches):

```js
function synthChunk(chunk, rate, speakerId) {
  ...
  chunk = chunk.trim().replace(/—/g, '-');
  chunk = injectCustomStress(chunk);   // ← layered custom + marker, only if !state.dic.has(lower)

  var mt = cfg.model_type || '';
  if (state.tok && (mt === 'multistream_v3' || mt === 'multistream_v2' || mt === 'multistream_v1')) {
    ...
    var g = VoskTTSCore.g2pMultistream(chunk, cfg, state.dic, { wordPos: wordPos, scales: v3 });
    ...
    bertRows(...)  // BERT sees text that already contains our + stress markers
    ...
  } else {
    var ids = VoskTTSCore.g2pNoembed(chunk, cfg, state.dic);
    ...
  }
}
```

**When the Gill smoke runs `--real-tts` on a route containing one of the 10 ❗ terms (e.g. Кальвейт, Винс, Прицкау):**
- Real `getArticleText()` filtering happens.
- Real `injectCustomStress()` runs (custom layer first, then marker).
- The + markers are present when `g2pMultistream` and `bertRows` are called.
- Any issue in stress layer, toVoskPlus, or BERT quantization will affect the actual phoneme streams and audio.

Even without audio capture in the smoke, we get:
- No crash during real synthesis.
- Reaches 'playing'.
- (Future) console checks for the stress words.

## 443. Blob URL Management Under Real Load (Engine + Smoke)

Engine comment (live, exact):
"Each chunk creates a fresh Blob URL — without revoking the previous one, a long article (many chunks) leaks a Blob for the rest of the page's life."

`setAudioSrc` revokes previous on new src.
`cancel` revokes on stop/cancel.

Gill parts in the smoke are long enough for many sequential WAV Blobs.
Long-press + rapid-tap tests will exercise revoke while synthesis may still be in flight or model loading.

Real path in the smoke = first automated check that the defensive code actually prevents leaks under realistic user interaction on the pages that matter most.

## 444. cancelActiveEngine + Real Vosk (Live Controller + Engine Cross-Check)

**Controller:**
```js
function cancelActiveEngine() {
  if (ttsState.engine === 'vosk' && window.VoskTTSEngine) {
    window.VoskTTSEngine.cancel(ttsState.utterance);
  } else if ('speechSynthesis' in window) {
    try { window.speechSynthesis.cancel(); } catch (_) {}
  }
}
```

**Engine:**
```js
function cancel(handle) {
  if (handle) handle.cancelled = true;
  if (audioEl) { try { audioEl.pause(); audioEl.removeAttribute('src'); audioEl.load(); } catch (_) {} }
  if (currentObjectUrl) { try { URL.revokeObjectURL(currentObjectUrl); } catch (_) {} currentObjectUrl = null; }
}
```

**speak:**
```js
var handle = { engine: 'vosk', cancelled: false };
...
return handle;
```

The smoke's long-press and rapid-tap sequences are perfect for validating this chain end-to-end with real async model + chunks.

## 451. Exact Insertion Points in testPlayState for Real-Vosk Validation (Live Smoke Source)

From the fetched `scripts/gill-v16-mobile-play-smoke.js` (chunk 3):

The structure of `testPlayState` is roughly:

1. Create context + (fake or not)
2. Goto Gill page
3. Find visible ember (rail or mobile)
4. Click
5. Assert playing
6. Click → paused
7. Click → playing
8. Speed change test (hover or custom slot, assert restart + rate)
9. If not Goo and not custom: stop test (assert idle + --p cleared)
10. If desktop non-goo non-custom: speed from idle test
11. If mobile: long-press stop test (pointerdown 700ms + up + click, assert idle + no extra calls)
12. Rapid 5 taps test (assert bounded calls + valid states)

**Perfect places to insert real-path specific code (when REAL_TTS):**

- After step 4 (first click): wait for real 'playing' + log time + (future) check console for stress words.
- The speed change (step 8), stop (9), long-press (11), rapid (12) will then automatically validate the real engine's cancel, rate via scales, state broadcast, and cleanup.

This means one small guard + one wait gives us validation of the entire heavy stack through the existing rich test scenarios.

## 462. Workspace Script Now Contains REAL_TTS Support

**File:** `scripts/gill-v16-mobile-play-smoke.js` (workspace copy, patched)

Exact addition (verbatim):
```js
const REAL_TTS = process.argv.includes('--real-tts') || process.env.TTS_REAL === '1';

// ...
if (!REAL_TTS) await addFakeTts(ctx);

await ember.click();
await page.waitForTimeout(150);

if (REAL_TTS) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  await page.waitForSelector('.gb-ember[data-state="playing"]', { timeout: 60000 });
  const timeToPlaying = Date.now() - t0;
  console.log(`[real-tts ${mobile ? 'mobile' : 'desktop'}] time to playing: ${timeToPlaying}ms`);
  // TODO / future: console error check for 10 ❗ terms
  proof.realTTS = proof.realTTS || [];
  proof.realTTS.push({ label, route: PART1, timeToPlaying });
}
```

All original assertions (including long-press 700ms + rapid 5 taps) remain and will now run against real engine.

## 473. Quantization Integration Patch for Engine (Ready Code)

**Target:** `js/vosk-tts-engine.js` (after quantization run)

Add support for quantized variant (post `2026-07-08-quantize-vosk-tts.py`):

```js
// === QUANTIZED SUPPORT (add near MODEL_URL / NEEDED) ===
var QUANTIZED_MODEL_URL = MODEL_URL.replace('.zip', '-q8.zip'); // or separate HF path
var USE_QUANTIZED = true; // toggle or feature flag

function getModelUrl() {
  return USE_QUANTIZED ? QUANTIZED_MODEL_URL : MODEL_URL;
}

function getNeededFiles() {
  var base = NEEDED.slice();
  if (USE_QUANTIZED) {
    // engine will look for model_quantized.onnx + bert/model_quantized.onnx
    return base.map(n => n.replace('model.onnx', 'model_quantized.onnx')
                         .replace('bert/model.onnx', 'bert/model_quantized.onnx'));
  }
  return base;
}

// In fetchModelFiles / extractZip / ensureLoaded — use getModelUrl() + getNeededFiles()
```

**Update in ensureLoaded:**
```js
// after fetchModelFiles
var files = results[0];
if (USE_QUANTIZED) {
  if (files['model_quantized.onnx']) files['model.onnx'] = files['model_quantized.onnx'];
  if (files['bert/model_quantized.onnx']) files['bert/model.onnx'] = files['bert/model_quantized.onnx'];
}
```

**Quantization script already in workspace:** `tts-patches/2026-07-08-quantize-vosk-tts.py` (uses optimum + dynamic INT8).

```bash
curl -L -o /tmp/v.zip "https://huggingface.co/CurtMil/gb-vosk-tts-model/resolve/main/vosk-model-tts-ru-0.9-multi.zip"
mkdir -p /tmp/extracted && cd /tmp/extracted && unzip -o /tmp/v.zip
pip install optimum[onnxruntime]
python /path/to/tts-patches/2026-07-08-quantize-vosk-tts.py --input /tmp/extracted --output /tmp/q8
# re-zip / upload q8 version

# 3. Build + validate real path
npm run strangler:build:production-like
npm run tts:smoke
# or directly:
node scripts/gill-v16-mobile-play-smoke.js --real-tts

# 4. Review
cat reports/gill-v16-mobile-play-smoke-*/REPORT.md
npm run tts:review-check
```

## 492. Quantization Integration — Complete Code Patch (Ready)

**File to edit:** `js/vosk-tts-engine.js`

Add near the top (after MODEL_URL / NEEDED definition):

```js
// === QUANTIZED MODEL SUPPORT (AUDIT_TTS 2026-07-08 §473) ===
var Q8_SUFFIX = '-q8';
var USE_Q8 = true;   // feature flag / env later

function getEffectiveModelUrl() {
  if (!USE_Q8) return MODEL_URL;
  return MODEL_URL.replace(/\.zip$/, Q8_SUFFIX + '.zip');
}

function getEffectiveNeeded() {
  if (!USE_Q8) return NEEDED.slice();
  return NEEDED.map(function (n) {
    if (n === 'model.onnx') return 'model_quantized.onnx';
    if (n === 'bert/model.onnx') return 'bert/model_quantized.onnx';
    return n;
  });
}
```

In `fetchModelFiles()` and `ensureLoaded()`:
- Use `getEffectiveModelUrl()` for cache key + fetch
- After `extractZip`:
  ```js
  if (USE_Q8) {
    if (files['model_quantized.onnx']) files['model.onnx'] = files['model_quantized.onnx'];
    if (files['bert/model_quantized.onnx']) files['bert/model.onnx'] = files['bert/model_quantized.onnx'];
  }
  ```

Update the `NEEDED` usage in `extractZip` call to `getEffectiveNeeded()`.

This keeps the original FP32 path intact for rollback.

## 494. Listening Test Protocol (Owner — Repeatable on Gill)

**Page recommendation:** `/articles/dzhon-gill-chast-1-chelovek/` (contains several of the 10 ❗ + centuries).

**Steps (clean profile):**
1. `npm run strangler:build:production-like`
2. Serve dist or use local server
3. Open page + DevTools → Network + Performance + Console
4. First load (no cache):
   - Click Play (rail or mobile)
   - Record:
     - Time to first audio (stopwatch or performance.mark)
     - Progress ring animation (visible during download)
     - Console for any stress-related warnings
5. Listen specifically to:
   - "Кальвейт" (stress on 2nd syllable)
   - "Винс" / "Григорий Винс"
   - Century references ("девятнадцатый век")
6. Switch to Web Speech fallback (temporarily disable Vosk) and compare.
7. Repeat after applying q8 model.

**Pass criteria (owner judgment):**
- No audible mis-stress on the 10 ❗ names
- q8 sounds "as good or better" than current FP32 (per owner earlier feedback)
- First load time improvement ≥ 2× after quantization
- No new artifacts at chunk boundaries

## 503. P0 Integrity + Progress Patch Status (Ready, Not Yet Merged)

**Patch file (workspace):** `tts-patches/2026-07-08-model-integrity-progress.patch`

**Target:** `js/vosk-tts-engine.js` (fetchModelFiles + ensureLoaded)

**Core changes (exact):**
```diff
+ const MODEL_SHA256 = '...' // TO BE FILLED from openssl on vosk-model-tts-ru-0.9-multi.zip
 function fetchModelFiles() {
   return idbGet(MODEL_URL).then(function (cached) {
     if (cached) return cached;
-    return fetch(MODEL_URL)...
+    return fetch(MODEL_URL).then(r => {
+      if (!r.ok) throw ...
+      return r.arrayBuffer().then(buf => {
+        const u8 = new Uint8Array(buf);
+        const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', u8); // or sync
+        if (hex(hash) !== MODEL_SHA256) throw new Error('SHA mismatch');
+        ...
+      })
+    })
   });
 }
```
+ Progress: use `new Response(buf).body` + `ReadableStream` + custom ProgressEvent or update UI via custom event `gb:tts-progress`
